10 Image Adjustment


## **Chapter 1. Overview**
### **1.1. Description**

**image.cgi** configures camera features such as SSDR, white balance, exposure and OSD. This document

describes how to configure each function in detail.


The following submenus are used for image settings.


 - **camera** : Configures various settings such as BLC, HLC, Iris, SSNR, etc.


 - **whitebalance** : Configures the relative amount of red, green and blue primary colors in the image so

that neutral colors are reproduced correctly.


 - **imageenhancements** : Adjusts the image sharpness, brightness, saturation, etc.


 - **imageenhancements2** : Image setting can be configured with imagepresetmode.


 - **irled** : Configures IR LED settings.


 - **flip** : Flips the image horizontally/vertically.


 - **ssdr** : Configures the SSDR (Samsung Super Dynamic Range) settings.


 - **focus** : Changes the focus settings according to the environment.


 - **overlay** : Configures OSD (On-screen Display) on the image.


 - **multilineosd** : Configures multiple lines of OSD (On-screen Display) on the image.


 - **multiimageosd:** Supports overlaying images on the live stream.


 - **smartcodec** : Configures smart codec settings.


 - **privacy** : Specifies a certain area of the video to be protected for privacy.


 - **fisheyelens** : Configures the settings for fisheye lenses.


 - **fisheyesetup** : Configures the settings, mount position and view modes for fisheye lenses.


 - **viewmodes** : Specifies the viewing mode.


 - **imagepreset** : Configures the image preset schedule settings.


 - **imagepreset2** : Configures each image preset mode.


 - **imagealignment** : Configures the image alignment of multiple sensors.


 - **imageoptions** : Explains the dependency between various features in image cgi.


 - **ptr** : Configures the PTR settings.


 - **ptrzusage** : Provides information on the number of times the PTR feature was used, to track durability.


 - **autoimagealignment** : Adjusts panoramic camera’s panoramic image.


 - **imagepresetschedule** : Configures image preset mode’s scheduling,


 - **thermalpalettesetting** : Configures the thermal color palette, upper/lower temperature level,

emissivity, and color bar overlay.


 - **thermalpalettesettingoptions** : Provides temperature unit’s configurable temperature range.


SUNAPI 11


 - **spottemperaturereading** : Provides requested spot’s temperature information.


 - **directionindicator** : Configures settings about direction indicator.


 - **noisereduct** : Provides features related image quality control for an analog camera connected to a

Hybrid NVR.


 - **ptrpreset** : Provides features that can configure settings for ptr presets.


 - **stereosensorcalibration** : Calibrates the coordinates of both sensors, which is performed by

superimposing a thermal image over a non-thermal image based on the scale and adjustment of the

offset.


 - **radiometrysettings** : Configures radiometry settings for the camera.


 - **radiometrysettingsoptions** : Requests information about radiometry settings.


 - **blackbodyconfig** : Configures the use of black body device for the camera.


 - **blackbodyconfigoptions** : Requests information about black body settings.


 - **focuspreset** : Supports how to configure presets for each focus.


 - **thermalnuc** : Thermal camera non uniformity correction settings.


 - **lpcapturesetup** : Supports to install camera for LPR capturing.


 - **wiseautofocus** : Wiseautofocus related settings.


 - **autofocuszone** : autofocus zone setup.


 - **whiteled** : Configures White LED settings.


12 Image Adjustment


## **Chapter 2. Camera**
### **2.1. Description**

The **camera** submenu configures various settings, such as BLC, HLC, Iris, SSNR, etc.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|User|
|set|User|


### **2.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 camera &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **2.3. Parameters**

```













|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads the camera settings such as<br>BLC, HLC, SSNR, etc.|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
||LensModel|RES|<enum><br>SLA-T46XX,<br>SLA-T10XX,<br>SLA-T24XX|Lens models of the camera<br>This parameter is only shown in case<br>of model that support changing lens.<br>**Note**<br>It is not applied for the fisheye<br>camera.|

|---|---|---|---|---|

These two parameters<br>should not be set together.<br>**Note**<br>Each ImagePresetMode.# can<br>have different image settings. For<br>more details, refer to the<br>"ImagePresetMode" parameter in<br>set actions.|
||ImagePresetMode.#.LCE<br>Mode|RES|<enum><br>Full,<br>Horizontal,<br>Sky,<br>Ground,<br>Center75%,<br>Center50%,<br>Center25%,<br>Custom|LCEMode<br>Mode can display the screen with a<br>high contrast based on the set area.<br>• Full: Full screen.<br>• Horizontal: Rectangular area in the<br>middle of the screen.<br>• Sky: Rectangular area at the upper<br>part of the screen<br>• Ground: Rectangular area at the<br>lower part of the screen<br>• Center75%: 75% Area size from the<br>center<br>• Center50%: 50% Area size from the<br>center<br>• Center25%: 25% Area size from the<br>center<br>• Custom: If LCEMode is set to this,<br>LCETop, LCEBottom, LCELeft,<br>LCERight parameters can be set.|

||DayNightMode|REQ, RES|<enum><br>Color, BW,<br>Auto,<br>ExternalBW<br>, Schedule|Day and night mode<br>The controls the IR-cut filter if one is<br>available on the device.<br>• Color: IR-cut filter is on; always<br>outputs in color.<br>• BW: IR-cut filter is off; always<br>outputs in black and white.<br>• Auto: IR-cut filter is automatically<br>on or off based on the light level;<br>normally outputs in color but will<br>output black and white under low<br>luminance at night.<br>• ExternalBW: Controls the color of<br>the video when the alarm input<br>terminal is synchronized with an<br>external device.<br>• Schedule: Outputs in color only in<br>the specified time.<br>If**AGCMode** is set to Off,<br>**DayNightMode** cannot be set to<br>Auto.<br>**Note**<br>Attribute to check for DayNight:<br>"attributes/Image/Support/DayNi<br>ght"|

## **Chapter 3. White Balance**
### **3.1. Description**

The **whitebalance** submenu configures white balance settings.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


Attribute to check for White Balance support: "attributes/Image/Support/WhiteBalance"


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|User|
|set|User|
|control|User|


### **3.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 whitebalance &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **3.3. Parameters**

```








|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads white balance settings|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|


96 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Whi<br>teBalanceMode|RES|<enum><br>Manual,<br>AWC, ATW,<br>Outdoor,<br>Indoor,<br>ATW1,<br>ATW2,<br>3200K,<br>5600K,<br>Mercury,<br>Sodium,<br>NarrowATW|White balance mode<br>• Manual: Manually adjust the red<br>and blue gains of the camera<br>video.<br>• AWC: Corrects the colors of the<br>camera video to be optimized to<br>the current lighting condition and<br>screen mode.<br>• ATW: Automatically corrects the<br>colors of the camera video.<br>(2400K~10500K)<br>• Outdoor: Automatically corrects<br>the video colors of the camera to<br>be optimized to the outdoor<br>environment. (1800K~10500K)<br>• Indoor: Automatically corrects the<br>video colors of the camera to be<br>optimized to the indoor<br>environment. (4500K~8500K)<br>• Mercury: Automatically corrects<br>the video colors of the camera to<br>be optimized to a mercury lamp<br>environment.<br>• Sodium: Automatically corrects the<br>video colors of the camera to be<br>optimized to a sodium lamp<br>environment.<br>• NarrowATW: Automatically<br>corrects the video colors of the<br>camera to be optimized to current<br>lighting condition and screen<br>mode in a narrower range than<br>ATW mode (2800K~9000K)<br>**Note**<br>Each ImagePresetMode.# can<br>have different image settings. For<br>more details, refer to the<br>"ImagePresetMode" parameter in<br>set actions.|



SUNAPI 97


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Whi<br>teBalanceManualRedLev<br>el|RES|<int>|Red level of the white balance<br>The higher the value, the greater the<br>red level.<br>**WhiteBalanceManualRedLevel** is<br>valid only when**WhiteBalanceMode**<br>is set to Manual.|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Whi<br>teBalanceManualBlueLev<br>el|RES|<int>|Blue level of the white balance.<br>The higher the value, the greater the<br>blue level.<br>**WhiteBalanceManualBlueLevel** is<br>valid only when**WhiteBalanceMode**<br>is set to Manual.|
|set|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||ImagePreview|REQ|<enum><br>Start, Stop|Image preview mode<br>Allows the user to view a preview<br>image of the configuration, rather<br>than saving the image configuration<br>to the camera.<br>If this parameter is ignored, then<br>preview mode will be stopped and the<br>original image configuration will be<br>applied.<br>• Start: Image preview mode will be<br>started<br>• Stop: Image preview mode will be<br>stopped and the original image<br>settings saved in the camera will<br>be applied.<br>**CAMERA ONLY**<br>|



98 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||WhiteBalanceMode|REQ, RES|<enum><br>Manual,<br>AWC, ATW,<br>Outdoor,<br>Indoor,<br>ATW1,<br>ATW2,<br>3200K,<br>5600K,<br>Mercury,<br>Sodium,<br>NarrowATW|White balance mode<br>• Manual: Adjusts the Red and blue<br>gains of the camera video<br>manually<br>• AWC: Corrects the colors of the<br>camera video to be optimized to<br>the current lighting condition and<br>screen mode (2400K~10500K)<br>• ATW: Corrects the colors of the<br>camera video automatically<br>• Outdoor: Automatically corrects<br>the video colors of the camera to<br>be optimized to the outdoor<br>environment (1800K~10500K)<br>• Indoor: Automatically corrects the<br>video colors of the camera to be<br>optimized to the indoor<br>environment. (4500K~8500K)<br>• Mercury: Automatically corrects<br>the video colors of the camera to<br>be optimized to a mercury lamp<br>environment.<br>• Sodium: Automatically corrects the<br>video colors of the camera to be<br>optimized to a sodium lamp<br>environment.<br>• NarrowATW: Automatically<br>corrects the video colors of the<br>camera to be optimized to current<br>lighting condition and screen<br>mode in a narrower range than<br>ATW mode (2800K~9000K)|
||WhiteBalanceManualRed<br>Level|REQ, RES|<int>|Red level of the white balance<br>The higher the value, the greater the<br>red level.<br>**WhiteBalanceManualRedLevel** is<br>valid only when**WhiteBalanceMode**<br>is set to Manual.|



SUNAPI 99


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||WhiteBalanceManualBlu<br>eLevel|REQ, RES|<int>|Blue level of the white balance.<br>The higher the value, the greater the<br>blue level.<br>**WhiteBalanceManualBlueLevel** is<br>valid only when**WhiteBalanceMode**<br>is set to Manual.|
||ImagePresetMode|REQ|<enum><br>UserPreset<br>1,<br>UserPreset<br>2,<br>OutdoorDa<br>ytime,<br>OutdoorNig<br>httime,<br>IndoorBack<br>light,<br>IndoorBrig<br>htScene,<br>NumberPla<br>te, Vivid,<br>Indoor,<br>Outdoor|Each ImagePresetMode can have<br>ImagePreset settings.<br>Image settings in any Mode can be<br>changed. Default settings for<br>UserPreset1 and UserPreset2 are the<br>same, whereas other preset mode like<br>OutdoorDaytime,<br>OutdoorNighttime,etc., have specif<br>default settings depending on its<br>name.|
|control|AWCSet|||AWC mode set<br>This parameter is used for a one-time<br>control action to obtain the optimal<br>conditions for the current lighting. It<br>does not require any value.<br>This parameter is valid only when<br>**WhiteBalanceMode** is set to AWC.|

```

REQUEST 2

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=whitebalance&action=control&AWCSet

```

## **Chapter 4. Image Enhancements**
### **4.1. Description**

The **imageenhancements** submenu configures the image enhancement settings.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|Encoder|NVR|
|---|---|---|---|
|view|User|Admin|User|
|set|User|Admin|User|
|control|User|Admin||


### **4.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 imageenhancements &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **4.3. Parameters**

```













|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads image enhancement settings|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
|set|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||OpticalDefogFilterEnable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables the optical defog<br>filter.|



SUNAPI 103


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||ImagePreview|REQ|<enum><br>Start, Stop|Image preview mode<br>Allows the user to view a preview<br>image of the configuration, rather<br>than saving the image configuration<br>to the camera.<br>If this parameter is ignored, then<br>preview mode will be stopped and the<br>original image configuration will be<br>applied.<br>• Start: Image preview mode will be<br>started<br>• Stop: Image preview mode will be<br>stopped, and the original image<br>settings saved in the camera will<br>be applied.<br>**CAMERA ONLY**<br>|
||SharpnessEnable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables image sharpness<br>**CAMERA ONLY**<br> <br>**Note**<br>Attribute to check for Sharpness:<br>"attributes/Image/Support/Sharp<br>ness"|

||GammaControl|REQ, RES|<bool>|Gamma Control<br>Adjust the brightness of the image.<br>When On is selected, dark areas are<br>generally brighter.<br>**CAMERA ONLY**<br> <br>**Note**<br>When setting On, the screen<br>change due to changes in<br>brightness and gamma items may<br>not be significant.|
||Saturation|REQ, RES|<int>|Saturation level<br>The higher the value, the greater the<br>saturation.<br>**CAMERA ONLY**<br> <br>**Note**<br>Attribute to check for Saturation:<br>"attributes/Image/Support/Satura<br>tion"|

## **Chapter 5. IR LED**
### **5.1. Description**

The **irled** submenu configures IR LED (Infra-red Light-Emitting-Diode) settings. IR LED creates brighter

and clearer images in low illumination situations.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


Attribute to check for Feature Support: "attributes/Image/Support/IRLED"


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|User|
|set|User|


### **5.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 irled &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **5.3. Parameters**

```








|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads IR LED settings.|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
|set|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|


112 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||ImagePreview|REQ|<enum><br>Start, Stop|Image preview mode<br>Allows the user to view a preview<br>image of the configuration, rather<br>than saving the image configuration<br>to the camera.<br>If this parameter is ignored, then<br>preview mode will be stopped, and the<br>original image configuration will be<br>applied.<br>• Start: Image preview mode will be<br>started<br>• Stop: Image preview mode will be<br>stopped, and the original image<br>settings saved in the camera will<br>be applied.|
||Mode|REQ, RES|<enum><br>Off, On,<br>Sensor,<br>Schedule,<br>Manual,<br>Auto,<br>Auto1,<br>Auto2|IR LED mode<br>• Off: Turns off IR LED.<br>• On: Turns on IR LED.<br>• Sensor: Changes the IR according<br>to the light flux detected by the<br>light sensor.<br>• Schedule: Sets the IR usage time.<br>• Manual: Controls the IR manually.<br>• Auto, Auto1, Auto2: Controls the IR<br>Level automatically. See product<br>specs for detailed information on<br>the actual differences between the<br>modes|
||LEDOnLevel|REQ, RES|<int>|LED On level<br>LED is turned on when the light flux is<br>lower than the LED usage level.<br>**LEDOnLevel** is valid only when**Mode**<br>is set to Sensor.|


SUNAPI 113


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||LEDOffLevel|REQ, RES|<int>|LED Off level<br>LED is turned off when the light flux is<br>higher than the LED usage level.<br>**LEDOffLevel** is valid only when**Mode**<br>is set to Sensor.|
||LEDPowerControlMode|REQ, RES|<enum><br>Off, Auto|LED power control<br>• Off: Turns off the LED power<br>control.<br>• Auto: Controls the screen<br>saturation caused by a nearby<br>object.|
||LEDMaxPower|REQ, RES|<enum><br>Low,<br>Medium,<br>High|Maximum power for IR LED<br>It sets the highest brightness of IR.|
||LEDMaxPowerLevel|REQ, RES|<int>|Maximum power level for IR LED<br>It sets the highest IR brightness|
||Schedule.EveryDay.From<br>To|REQ, RES|<string>|Schedule for IR LED<br>The schedule sets the time when the<br>IR will remain turned on in the format<br>of <hh:mm-hh:mm>.<br>**Schedule.EveryDay.FromTo** is valid<br>only when**Mode** is set to Sensor.|
||Level|REQ, RES|<int>|IR LED level<br>**Level** is valid only when**Mode** is set to<br>Manual|
||Zone.#.Level|REQ, RES|<int>|IR LED level by zone<br>It can be set if**Mode** is set as Manual.|

## **Chapter 6. Flip**
### **6.1. Description**

The **flip** submenu configures image flip settings.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|User|
|set|User|


### **6.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 flip &action=<value>&[<parameter>=<value>]

### **6.3. Parameters**

```










|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads image flip settings.|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
|set|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||HorizontalFlipEnable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables horizontal image<br>flipping.|
||VerticalFlipEnable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables vertical image<br>flipping.|
||Rotate|REQ, RES|<enum><br>0, 90, 270|Rotates a video by a specified angle|

### **6.4. Examples**

```

```

```

 Content-type: text/plain
 <Body>

 Channel.0.HorizontalFlipEnable=False
 Channel.0.VerticalFlipEnable=False
 Channel.0.Rotate=0

```

JSON RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK

 Content-type: application/json

 {
 "Channel": 0,
 "HorizontalFlipEnable": false,

 "Rotate": "0"
 }
 ]
 }

## **Chapter 7. SSDR**
### **7.1. Description**

The **SSDR** submenu configures the SSDR (Samsung Super Dynamic Range) settings. It regulates the

overall brightness by increasing the brightness of the dark area alone in a scene where the difference

between bright and dark is severe.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|User|
|set|User|


### **7.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 ssdr &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **7.3. Parameters**

```













|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads SSDR (Samsung Super Dynamic<br>Range) settings.|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Lev<br>el|RES|<int>|SSDR level<br>The higher the value, the greater the<br>SSDR level.<br>**Level** is valid only when**Enable** is set<br>to True.<br>**Note**<br>Each ImagePresetMode.# can<br>have different image settings. For<br>more details, refer to the<br>"ImagePresetMode" parameter in<br>set actions.|



SUNAPI 121


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Dyn<br>amicRange|RES|<enum><br>Narrow,<br>Wide|SSDR mode<br>**DynamicRange** is valid only when<br>**Enable** is set to True.|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Ena<br>ble|RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables SSDR.|
|set|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||ImagePresetMode|REQ|<enum><br>UserPreset<br>1,<br>UserPreset<br>2,<br>OutdoorDa<br>ytime,<br>OutdoorNig<br>htime,<br>IndoorBack<br>light,<br>IndoorBrig<br>htScene,<br>NumberPla<br>te, Vivid,<br>Indoor,<br>Outdoor|Each**ImagePresetMode** can have<br>ImagePreset settings.<br>Image settings in any Mode can be<br>changed, but modes except<br>UserPreset1 and UserPreset2 have<br>different default image settings.|
||ImagePreview|REQ|<enum><br>Start, Stop|Image preview mode<br>Allows the user to view a preview<br>image of the configuration, rather<br>than saving the image configuration<br>to the camera.<br>If this parameter is ignored, then<br>preview mode will be stopped, and the<br>original image configuration will be<br>applied.<br>• Start: Image preview mode will be<br>started<br>• Stop: Image preview mode will be<br>stopped, and the original image<br>settings saved in the camera will<br>be applied.|

||Enable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables SSDR.|
||Level|REQ, RES|<int>|SSDR level<br>The higher the value, the greater the<br>SSDR level.<br>**Level** is valid only when**Enable** is set<br>to True.|
||DynamicRange|REQ, RES|<enum><br>Narrow,<br>Wide|SSDR mode<br>**DynamicRange** is valid only when<br>**Enable** is set to True.|

## **Chapter 8. Focus**
### **8.1. Description**

The **focus** submenu configures the focus settings.


**NOTE** NVR supports only control action.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|NVR|
|---|---|---|
|view|User||
|set|User||
|control|User|User|


### **8.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 focus &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **8.3. Parameters**

```















|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads the focus settings.|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Fast<br>AutoFocus|RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables Fast Auto Focus<br>mode.<br>**Note**<br>Each ImagePresetMode.# can<br>have different image settings. For<br>more details, refer to the<br>"ImagePresetMode" parameter in<br>set actions.|
||ImagePresetMode.#.IRS<br>hift|RES|<enum><br>Off, 780,<br>850, 940|IR wave length setting|



SUNAPI 127


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Te<br>mperatureCompensation<br>Enable|RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables temperature<br>compensation.<br>This is only available when**IrisMode** is<br>set to ICS-EG3Z3915TCS-MPEIR.|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Mo<br>de|RES|<enum><br>Auto,<br>Manual,<br>OneShotAu<br>toFocus|Focus mode<br>• Auto: Adjusts the focus<br>automatically according to the<br>zoom operation.<br>• Manual: Manually Adjust the focus<br>according to the zoom operation.<br>• OneShotAutoFocus: Auto focus is<br>performed once after the zoom<br>operation.|

## **Chapter 9. Overlay**
### **9.1. Description**

The **overlay** submenu configures title and time OSD (on-screen display) settings.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|User|
|set|User|


### **9.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 overlay &action=<value>&[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **9.3. Parameters**

```















|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads title and time OSD (on-screen<br>display) settings|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
||ImagePresetMode.#.PTZ<br>PositionEnable|RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables the PTZ position<br>overlay<br>**Note**<br>Each ImagePresetMode.# can<br>have different image settings. For<br>more details, refer to the<br>"ImagePresetMode" parameter in<br>set actions.|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Pre<br>setNameEnable|RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables the preset name<br>overlay.|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Ca<br>meraIDEnable|RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables the camera ID<br>overlay.|



140 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Azi<br>muthEnable|RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables the azimuth<br>overlay.<br>**Note**<br>Attribute to check for Azimuth:<br>"attributes/PTZSupport/Support/<br>Azimuth"|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Qui<br>ckZoomAndFocusEnable|RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables the<br>QuickZoomAndFocus overlay.|
|Set|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||ImagePresetMode|REQ|<enum><br>UserPreset<br>1,<br>UserPreset<br>2,<br>OutdoorDa<br>ytime,<br>OutdoorNig<br>htime,<br>IndoorBack<br>light,<br>IndoorBrig<br>htScene,<br>NumberPla<br>te, Vivid,<br>Indoor,<br>Outdoor|Each**ImagePresetMode** can have<br>ImagePreset settings.<br>Image settings in any Mode can be<br>changed, but modes except<br>UserPreset1 and UserPreset2 have<br>different default image settings.|
||TitleEnable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables title overlay|
||Title|REQ, RES|<string>|Camera title<br>**Title** is valid only when**TitleEnable** is<br>set to True.|
||TitlePositionX|REQ, RES|<int>|Camera title overlay position row<br>The value may vary depending on the<br>model.<br>**TitlePositionX** and**TitlePositionY** are<br>valid only when**TitleEnable** is set to<br>True.|

||TimeEnable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables time overlay|
||TimeFormat|REQ, RES|<enum>|Time overlay format<br>The time can be specified in the<br>format of <YYYY-MM-DD>, <MM-DD-<br>YYYY>, or <DD-MM-YYYY>.<br>**TimeFormat** is valid only when the<br>**TimeEnable** is set to True.|

||WeekdayEnable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables the day of week<br>overlay<br>**WeekdayEnable** is valid only when<br>**TimeEnable** is set to True.|

## **Chapter 10. Multi Line Overlay**
### **10.1. Description**

The **multilineosd** submenu configures multiple OSD (on-screen display) title and time settings.


This chapter applies to network cameras only.

Attribute to check for maximum value of OSD title Supported:



**NOTE**


**Access level**



"attributes/Image/Limit/MaxOSDTitles"

Attribute to check for maximum value of OSD Date Supported:

"attributes/Image/Limit/MaxOSDDates"



|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|User|
|add/update|User|
|remove|User|

### **10.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 multilineosd &action=<value>&[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **10.3. Parameters**

```








|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads title and time OSD (on-screen<br>display) settings|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
||Index|REQ, RES|<csv>|OSD Index can be more than the sum<br>of**MaxOSDTitles** and**MaxOSDDate**.<br>It is a mandatory parameter.|


148 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Ind<br>ex.#.OSDType|RES|<enum><br>Date, Title|OSD Type is a mandatory parameter<br>**Note**<br>Each ImagePresetMode.# can<br>have different image settings. For<br>more details, refer to the<br>"ImagePresetMode" parameter in<br>set actions.|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Ind<br>ex.#.WeekDay|RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables the day of week<br>overlay.<br>**WeekDay** is valid only when**OSDType**<br>is set to Date.|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Ind<br>ex.#.DateFormat|RES|<enum><br>YYYY-MM-<br>DD, MM-<br>DD-YYYY,<br>DD-MM-<br>YYYY|Date overlay format<br>The time can be specified in any one<br>of the supported formats.<br>**DateFormat** is valid only when the<br>**OSDType** is set to Date.|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Ind<br>ex.#.OSD|RES|<string>|OSD text to be displayed on the image<br>OSD is valid only when**OSDType** is set<br>to Title.|
||ImagePresetMode.#.Ind<br>ex.#.OSDBlink|RES|<enum><br>OFF, HDMI|Enables or disables OSD blinking for<br>HDMI video output of the camera.|

## **Chapter 11. Multi Image Overlay**
### **11.1. Description**

The **multiimageosd** submenu supports overlaying images on the live stream.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|User|
|install|User|
|remove|User|


### **11.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 multiimageosd &action=<value>&[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **11.3. Parameters**

```








|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads Image overlay settings|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
||Index|REQ|<csv>|List of overlay image index|
||MaxResolution|RES|<string>|Maximum image resolution that can<br>be uploaded for image overlay|
||IsInstalled|RES|<bool>|Indicates if an image is already<br>installed or not|
|set|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||PositionX|REQ, RES|<int>|X Coordinate of the image|
||PositionY|REQ, RES|<int>|Y Coordinate of the image|
|install|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||Index|REQ, RES|<csv>|Overlay Image Index|


162 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||FileType|REQ, RES|<enum><br>BMP, GIF,<br>JPG, JPEG,<br>PNG, TIF|Specifies the format of the image that<br>should be installed|
|remove|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||Index|REQ, RES|<int>|Overlay Image Index|

### **11.4. Examples**

#### **11.4.1. Getting the current information of Channel 0**

REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=multiimageosd&action=view&Channel=0

```

TEXT RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK
 Content-type: text/plain
 <Body>

 Channel.0.Index.1.IsInstalled=True
 Channel.0.MaxResolution=128x128
 Channel.0.Index.1.PositionX=1086
 Channel.0.Index.1.PositionY=728

```

JSON RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK
 Content-type: application/json
 <Body>

 {
 "multiimageosd": [
 {
 "Channel": 0,
 "MaxResolution": "128x128",

```

SUNAPI 163


```

 {
 "Index": 1,
 "IsInstalled": true
 "PositionX": 1086,
 "PositionY": 728
 }

#### **11.4.2. Installing overlay image**
```

Make sure that the image you are uploading is in a format supported by the device.


Refer to the **image/multiimageosd/install/FileType** response in attributes.cgi for information on

supported file formats.


The maximum image resolution supported is provided in the **MaxResolution** parameter.


REQUEST

```
 *[.underline]#Step 1:#*

```

Image file needs to be base64 encoded before sending a POST request.

```
 > openssl base64 -in image.bmp -out image_base64.bin

```

**Step 2:**


curl -v --digest -u <userid>:<password> --data-urlencode @image_base64.bin "http://<Device IP>/stw
cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=multiimageosd&action=install&Index=1&FileType=BMP" -H "Expect:"


The above command will produce a request to the device that looks something like:

```
 POST /stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=multiimageosd&action=install&Index=1&FileType=BMP
 HTTP/1.1
 User-Agent: curl/7.26.0
 Host: 192.168.22.47
 Accept: */*
 Content-Length: 147610

```

164 Image Adjustment


```
 Content-Type: application/x-www-form-urlencoded

```

TEXT RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK

 Content-type: text/plain

 <Body>

 OK

```

JSON RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK
 Content-type: application/json
 <Body>

 {
 "Response": "Success"
 }

#### **11.4.3. Removing an installed overlay image**
```

REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=multiimageosd&action=remove&Channel=0&Index=1

```

TEXT RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK
 Content-type: text/plain
 <Body>

 OK

```

 }

```

## **Chapter 12. Smart Codec**
### **12.1. Description**

The **smartcodec** submenu configures smart codec settings.


Using Smart Codec, specific areas are compressed with high quality and other areas with low quality.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|
|set|Admin|
|add|Admin|
|remove|Admin|


### **12.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 smartcodec &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **12.3. Parameters**

```








|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads smart codec settings|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
|set|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|


SUNAPI 167


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||Mode|REQ, RES|<enum><br>FaceDetecti<br>on, Manual|Smart codec mode<br>• FaceDetection: Detects the face<br>and compresses it with high<br>quality<br>• Manual: Defines The area<br>(rectangle type) manually for high<br>quality compression<br>**Note**<br>**add** and**remove** actions are valid<br>only when**Mode** is set to Manual.|
||Sensitivity|REQ, RES|<int>|Face detection sensitivity<br>**Sensitivity** is valid only when**Mode** is<br>set to FaceDetection.|
||QualityLevel|REQ, RES|<enum>|Quality of user-defined smart codec<br>area or the face detection area|
|add|Channel|REQ|<int>|Channel ID|
||Area.#.Coordinate|REQ, RES|<string>|Area coordinates for smart codec<br>The coordinates should be in the<br>format of <x1,y1,x2,y2,x3,y3,x4>.<br>**Note**<br>To use the**add** action,**Mode** must<br>have been set to Manual, and<br>**Area.#.Coordinate** must be sent<br>together.<br>Attribute to check for maximum smart<br>codec area(s):<br>"attributes/Image/Limit/MaxSmartCo<br>decArea"|
|remove|Channel|REQ|<int>|Channel ID|


168 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||Area|REQ|<csv><br>All, #|Smart codec area number/index to be<br>deleted<br>This parameter is valid only if**Mode** is<br>set to Manual.<br>**Note**<br>To use the**remove** action,**Mode**<br>must have been set to Manual,<br>and**Area** must be sent together.|

```

#### **12.4.3. Adding smart codec area 1**
```

To add a smart codec area for high quality compression using the **add** action, the **Mode** parameter must

have already been set to Manual; If **Mode** is set to FaceDetection, an error will be returned to the

following request.


REQUEST

```

## **Chapter 13. Privacy**
### **13.1. Description**

The **privacy** submenu configures privacy mask settings.


This chapter applies to network cameras only.

Attribute to check for privacy mask zoom threshold:

"attributes/Image/Support/PrivacyMask.ZoomThreshold"



**NOTE**


**Access level**



Attribute to check for privacy mask color:

"attributes/Image/Support/Privacy.MaskColor.Global"

Attribute to check for maximum privacy masks: "attributes/Image/Limit/

MaxPrivacyMask"

NVR supports only control action.



|Action|Camera|NVR|
|---|---|---|
|view|User||
|add, update|User||
|set|User||
|remove|User||
|control||User|

### **13.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 privacy &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **13.3. Parameters**

```













|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads privacy mask settings.|
||Channel|REQ|<csv>|Channel ID|
||CommonMaskColor|RES|<enum><br>Gray,<br>Green, Red,<br>Blue, Black,<br>White|This parameter is supported when the<br>**Privacy.MaskColor.Global** parameter<br>is True.<br>This parameter is used to configure<br>color for all privacy masks.|



172 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||Position.Pan|RES|<float>|Pan value when the device supports<br>PTZ.|
||Position.Tilt|RES|<float>|Tilt value when the device supports<br>PTZ.|
|add|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||MaskIndex|RES|<int>|Index of the privacy area<br>The value may vary depending on the<br>model.<br>**MaskIndex** should not be sent while<br>adding a privacy mask area. The<br>MaskIndex value will be returned in<br>response to a**view** request.|
||MaskName|REQ, RES|<string>|Privacy area’s name<br>The name may consist of letters,<br>numbers, hyphens and periods.<br>**Note**<br>To use the**add** action,<br>**MaskName**, **MaskColor**, and<br>**MaskCoordinate** parameters<br>must be sent together.|
||MaskColor|REQ, RES|<enum><br>Gray,<br>Green, Red,<br>Blue, Black,<br>White|Color of the privacy area<br>**Note**<br>To use the**add** action,<br>**MaskName**, **MaskColor**, and<br>**MaskCoordinate** parameters<br>must be sent together.<br>**Note**<br>In some cameras, changing the<br>color of each mask is not<br>supported. If<br>**Privacy.MaskColor.Global**<br>parameter is False, then this<br>feature is available. If this feature<br>is not available, then<br>**CommonMaskColor** parameter is<br>used and this parameter is used as<br>Common MaskColor.|



SUNAPI 173


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||MaskCoordinate|REQ, RES|<string>|Vertices of the privacy area<br>**Note**<br>To use the**add** action,<br>**MaskName**, **MaskColor**, and<br>**MaskCoordinate** parameters<br>must be sent together.|
||ZoomThresholdEnable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables the zoom<br>threshold|
||AdjustMode|REQ|<enum><br>Start|When adding a privacy area with this<br>parameter, the privacy area will be<br>transparent until some times passes<br>or an update command comes with<br>**AdjustMode** as Stop.<br>**Note**<br>This parameter is supported when<br>the device has a zoom module.|
|update|MaskIndex|REQ|<int>|Masks the index number|
||ZoomThresholdEnable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables the zoom<br>threshold|
||AdjustMode|REQ|<enum><br>Start, Stop|To adjust a privacy area’s zoom status<br>without removing it. When this comes<br>as Start, the area will be transparent<br>until some times passes or an update<br>command comes as Stop.<br>**Note**<br>To use this parameter, the<br>**MaskIndex** parameter must be<br>sent together. This is supported<br>when the device has a zoom<br>module.|
|set|Enable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Whether to use a privacy area|
||Channel|REQ|<int>|ID of the channel that will use the<br>privacy function|


174 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||MaskPattern|REQ, RES|<enum><br>Solid,<br>Mosaic1,<br>Mosaic2,<br>Mosaic3,<br>Mosaic4|Masks the pattern|
||CurrentIndexOrder|REQ|<csv>|The current**MaskIndex** list<br>**Note**<br>To change the**MaskIndex** order,<br>the**NewIndexOrder** parameters<br>must be sent together.|
||NewIndexOrder|REQ|<csv>|New**MaskIndex** list for changing the<br>orders<br>**Note**<br>To change the**MaskIndex** order,<br>the**CurrentIndexOrder**<br>parameters must be sent together.|
||CornerMasking|REQ,RES|<bool>|Enables corner masking of video<br>image.|
|remove|Channel|REQ|<int>|ID of the channel to remove the<br>privacy area(s) from.|
||MaskIndex|REQ|<csv><br>All, #|Privacy area(s) to remove<br>**Note**<br>To use the**remove** action,<br>**MaskIndex** parameter must be<br>sent.|
|control|Channel|REQ|<int>|ID of the channel from which to<br>control the privacy area<br>**Note**<br>The control action is supported<br>only in PTZ models.|


SUNAPI 175


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||Mode|REQ|<enum><br>Start, Move|Privacy mask control mode<br>• Start: Moves the screen’s center to<br>the center of the drawn area.<br>• Move: Moves the screen’s center<br>to the center of the MaskIndex’s<br>area.|
||MaskIndex|REQ|<int>|Privacy area for which the PTZ<br>coordinates should be adjusted<br>This parameter should be sent only<br>when Mode is Move|
||MaskCoordinate|REQ|<string>|Co-ordinates of the privacy mask that<br>is to be drawn. This parameter is used<br>to request PTZ camera to move so<br>that the Privacy area is at the center of<br>the scene.|

#### **13.4.2. Enabling a privacy area**
```

REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=privacy&action=set&Enable=True

#### **13.4.3. Disabling a privacy area**
```

REQUEST

178 Image Adjustment


#### **13.4.4. Setting the privacy protection area**

To use the **add** action, **MaskName**, **MaskColor**, and **MaskCoordinate** parameters must be set together. If

the mask area is a polygon type, the coordinate format is <x1,y1,x2,y2,x3,y3,x4,y4> and for a rectangle

type, the coordinate format is <x1,y1,x2,y2>.


REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=privacy&action=add&MaskColor=White&MaskCoordinate=70,
 140,817,807,1000,697,1140,420&MaskName=stwcamera

#### **13.4.5. Setting a privacy mask pattern**
```

REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=privacy&action=set&MaskPattern=Solid

#### **13.4.6. Changing the mask index at the user’s request**
```

To reorder the current mask indexes, **CurrentIndexOrder** and **NewIndexOrder** must have valid values

based on the **view** action. As the result of the below command, the 5th privacy mask will be returned

asthe 1st mask, and the 4th privacy mask will be returned as the 2nd mask, and so on.


REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=privacy&action=set&currentIndexOrder=1,3,4,5&NewIndex
 Order=5,4,1,3

#### **13.4.7. Removing the privacy protection area**
```

To use the **remove** action, the **MaskIndex** parameter must be sent. The mask index value can be

requested with the view action.


REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=privacy&action=remove&MaskIndex=1

#### **13.4.8. Adding a privacy mask with zoom threshold enabled**
```

On enabling a privacy mask with the zoom threshold feature, privacy masks are not displayed between

the zoom wide position and threshold position.


SUNAPI 179


REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=privacy&action=add&MaskName=M3&MaskColor=Black&MaskCo
 ordinate=100,100,200,200&ZoomThresholdEnable=True

#### **13.4.9. Adding a privacy mask when adjusting zoom threshold**
```

Before SUNAPI version 2.6.1, when enabling a privacy mask with the zoom threshold feature, privacy

masks were not displayed between the zoom wide position and the threshold position. With the new

parameter **AdjustMode**, the mask will be displayed as transparent until an update command with

**AdjustMode** as Stop is received or a specific time passes. Then the mask will change to a solid color

automatically.


REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=privacy&action=add&MaskIndex=1&MaskName=M3&MaskColor=
 Black&MaskCoordinate=100,100,200,200&ZoomThresholdEnable=True&AdjustMode=Sta
 rt

```

REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=privacy&action=update&MaskIndex=1&ZoomThresholdEnable
 =True&AdjustMode=Stop

#### **13.4.10. Adding a privacy mask in PTZ model using control action**
```

To move the PTZ camera, so that the privacy area being drawn is shown in the middle of the image, Mode

and MaskCoordinate parameter must be sent together.

#### **13.4.11. Setting the image preset**

To set the image preset with the **set** action, **Schedule.#.Mode** and **Schedule.#.EveryDay.FromTo** must

be set together. (If **ScheduleEnable** had been already set to True, the **ScheduleEnable** parameter could

be removed for the following request.)


REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=imagepreset&action=set&ScheduleEnable=True&Schedule.2
 .Mode=DefinitionFocus&Schedule.2.EveryDay.FromTo=00:00-00:01

```

180 Image Adjustment

|---|---|---|---|---|
||Schedule.#.EveryDay.<br>FromTo|REQ, RES|<string>|Start time for the image preset<br>The value is specified in the<br><hh:mm-hh:mm> format.<br>**Schedule.#.Mode** and<br>**Schedule.#.EveryDay.FromTo**<br>parameters are applicable only<br>when**ScheduleEnable** is set to<br>True.<br>**Note**<br>To use the**set** action,<br>**Schedule.#.Mode** and<br>**Schedule.#.EveryDay.FromT**<br>**o** must be sent together.|

#### **17.4.2. Setting the image preset**
```

To set the image preset with the **set** action, **Schedule.#.Mode** and **Schedule.#.EveryDay.FromTo** must

be set together. (If **ScheduleEnable** had been already set to True, the **ScheduleEnable** parameter could

be removed for the following request.)


REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=imagepreset&action=set&ScheduleEnable=True&Schedule.2
 .Mode=DefinitionFocus&Schedule.2.EveryDay.FromTo=00:00-00:01

```

196 Image Adjustment


## **Chapter 18. Image Alignment**
### **18.1. Description**

The **imagealignment** submenu configures the image alignment of multiple sensors.


This chapter applies to network cameras only.
**NOTE**

Attribute to check for multiple sensor support: "attributes/Image/Support/MultiImager"


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|
|set|Admin|
|control|Admin|


### **18.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 imagealignment &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **18.3. Parameters**

```













|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view|SensorID|RES|<int>||
|set|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||AlphaBlending|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enable or disable the Alpha Blending<br>feature of all sensors of a multi-<br>imager camera|



SUNAPI 197


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||AlphaBlendingLevel|REQ, RES|<int>|Level when**AlphaBlending** is<br>enabled.<br>When this value is greater than 0, the<br>overlapping image between 2 sensors<br>will be larger.<br>**Note**<br>To check if this feature is<br>supported, refer to the values of<br>the following attributes<br>"attributes/Image/Support/Image<br>Alignment"|
||FovAngle|REQ, RES|<enum><br>180Degree,<br>209Degree|Field of view angle|
|control|Channel|REQ|<int>|Channel ID|
||Mode|REQ|<enum><br>Move,<br>Reset|Image preset mode<br>• Move: Changes the sensor<br>calibration<br>• Reset : Resets the sensor<br>calibration|
||SensorID|REQ|<int>|Senor ID for which calibration needs<br>to be applied<br>**Note**<br>For information on the Maximum<br>number of sensors supported,<br>refer to the following CGI section<br>in attributes<br>**image/imagealignment/control/**<br>**SensorID**|


198 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||Horizontal|REQ|<int>|Distance the image should be<br>adjusted in horizontal direction. Value<br>is Normalized to a range of –N to N,<br>with negative values indicating the<br>image should be adjusted in the<br>leftward direction.<br>A positive value indicates the image<br>should be adjusted in the rightward<br>direction.<br>**Note**<br>For information on the actual<br>range of the normalized value,<br>refer to the following CGI section<br>in attributes<br>**image/imagealignment/control/Ho**<br>**rizontal**|
||Vertical|REQ|<int>|Distance the image should be<br>adjusted in vertical direction. Value is<br>Normalized to a range of –N to N; with<br>negative values indicating the image<br>should be adjusted in an upward<br>direction.<br>A positive value indicates the image<br>should be adjusted in a downward<br>direction.<br>**Note**<br>For information on the actual<br>range of the normalized value,<br>refer to the following CGI section<br>in attributes<br>image/imagealignment/control/Ve<br>rtical|

### **18.4. Examples**

#### **18.4.1. Aligning the images from different sensors**

To adjust the image by 5 units in a rightward direction and 5 units in an upward direction, the following

command can be used.


SUNAPI 199


REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=imagealignment&action=control&Channel=0&Mode=Move&Sen
 sorID=1&Horizontal=5&Vertical=-5&Channel=0

```

TEXT RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK

 Content-type: text/plain

 <Body>

 OK

```

JSON RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK

 Content-type: application/json

 <Body>

 {
 "Response": "Success"
 }

```

200 Image Adjustment


## **Chapter 19. Image Options**
### **19.1. Description**

The **imageoptions** submenu is used to explain the dependency between various features in image cgi.


This chapter applies to network cameras only.



**NOTE**


**Access level**



Attribute to check for Fisheye lens Support: "attributes/Image/Support/FisheyeLens"

Attribute to check for OSD Font Size Support: "image/multilineosd/add/FontSize"



|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|

### **19.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 imageoptions &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **19.3. Parameters**

```













|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view|Channel|REQ,RES|<csv>|Channel ID list|
||CameraPosition|RES|<enum><br>Wal|This parameter will be supported<br>only in Fisheye models.<br>**CameraPosition** and<br>**ViewModeType** parameters are<br>dependent. When camera<br>position is changed, the<br>supported view modes will<br>change.|

## **Chapter 20. PTR**
### **20.1. Description**

The **ptr** submenu configures the PTR settings.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|
|control|Admin|


### **20.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 ptr &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **20.3. Parameters**

```








|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads the focus settings.|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
||PanAngle|RES|<int>|Sets the Pan angle of the lens.<br>**Note**<br>Supported range may differ<br>depending on model.|
||TiltAngle|RES|<int>|Sets the Tilt angle of the lens.<br>**Note**<br>Supported range may differ<br>depending on model.|
||RotateAngle|RES|<int>|Sets the rotation angle of lens.<br>**Note**<br>Supported range may differ<br>depending on model.|
|control|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|


244 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||AutoRotate|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables the auto rotation<br>feature of a PTRZ camera.|
||Pan|REQ, RES|<int>|Panning to specified angle.<br>This is different from a normal PTZ<br>camera’s Pan operation. The purpose<br>of PTR differs.|
||Tilt|REQ, RES|<int>|Tilt to specified angle.<br>This is different from a normal PTZ<br>camera’s Pan operation. The purpose<br>of PTR differs.|
||Rotate|RES|<int>|Rotate to specified angle.|
||Mode|REQ|<enum><br>Continuous,<br>Relative,<br>Reset|If set to Relative, PTR moves in relative<br>mode.<br>If set to Reset, it returns to the initial<br>position.<br>If the value is not set by user, it moves<br>in continuous mode by default.|

### **20.4. Examples**




#### **20.4.1. Getting the current information of Channel 0**

REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=ptr&action=view&Channel=0

```

JSON RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK
 Content-type: application/json
 <Body>

 {
 "ptr": [
 {
 "Channel": 0,
 "PanAngle": 20,

```

SUNAPI 245


```
 "TiltAngle": 12,
 "RotateAngle": 40
 }
 ]
 }

#### **20.4.2. Controlling Pan, Tilt and Rotate position**
```

REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=ptr&action=control&Channel=0&Pan=50&Tilt=50&Rotate=50
 &Mode=Continuous

#### **20.4.3. Controlling Auto Rotate**
```

REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/ stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=ptr&action=control&Channel=0&AutoRotate=True

```

246 Image Adjustment


## **Chapter 21. PTRZ Usage**
### **21.1. Description**

The **ptrzusage** submenu provides information on the number of times the PTR feature was used, to track

durability.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|


### **21.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 ptrzusage &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **21.3. Parameters**

```








|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads the PTR usage information|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
||Pan|RES|<int>|Number of times Pan was used|
||Tilt|RES|<int>|Number of times Tilt was used|
||Rotate|RES|<int>|Number of times Rotate was used|

### **21.4. Examples**

#### **21.4.1. Getting the current information of Channel 0**

REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=ptrzusage&action=view&Channel=0

```

JSON RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK

```

SUNAPI 247


```
 Content-type: application/json

 <Body>

 {
 "ptr": [
 {
 "Channel": 0,
 "Pan": 0,
 "Tilt": 0,
 "Rotate": 4
 }
 ]
 }

```

248 Image Adjustment


## **Chapter 22. Image Enhancements 2**
### **22.1. Description**

The **imageenhancements2** submenu configures the image enhancement settings with image preset

mode.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|User|
|set|User|
|control|User|


### **22.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 imageenhancements2 &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **22.3. Parameters**

```















|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads image enhancement<br>settings|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
||ImagePresetMode.#.S<br>harpnessEnable|RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables image<br>sharpness<br>**Note**<br>Attribute to check for<br>Sharpness:<br>"attributes/Image/Support/S<br>harpness"<br>Each ImagePresetMode.# can<br>have different image setting. For<br>more details, refer the<br>"ImagePresetMode" parameter in<br>set action.|



SUNAPI 249


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||ImagePresetMode.#.S<br>harpnessLevel|RES|<int>|Sharpness level<br>**SharpnessLevel** is valid only<br>when**SharpnessEnable** is set to<br>True.|
||ImagePresetMode.#.B<br>rightness|RES|<int>|Brightness level<br>The higher the value, the greater<br>the brightness.<br>**Note**<br>Attribute to check for<br>Brightness:<br>"attributes/Image/Support/Br<br>ightness"|

## **Chapter 23. Auto Image Alignment**
### **23.1. Description**

The **autoimagealignment** submenu is newly added for PNM-9030V.


Check channel based AutoImageAlignmentSupport attribute to check if a particular
**NOTE**

channel supports it.


In case of PNM-9030V, only the first channel (panorama channel) supports this feature.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|
|set|Admin|


### **23.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 autoimagealignment &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **23.3. Parameters**

```













|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID of panoramic channel|
|set|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID<br>A camera uses from ‘0’ to max fixed<br>channel count-1 for channel ID.|
||FovDistance|REQ, RES|<float><br>1.5-100|Distance in meters|
||FovAngle|REQ, RES|<string><br>180Degree,<br>220Degree|Field of view angle|
|check|Status|RES|<enum><br>Idle,<br>InProgress,<br>Complete||



264 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||Channel|REQ, RES|||

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=autoimagealignment&action=view

```

 Channel.0.FovAngle=180Degree

```

## **Chapter 24. Image Preset 2**
### **24.1. Description**

The **imagepreset2** submenu configures the image preset mode, ImagePreview.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|User|
|set|User|
|control|User|


### **24.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 imagepreset2 &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **24.3. Parameters**

```








|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads settings for the image preset|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
|set|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|


SUNAPI 267


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||ImagePreview|REQ|<enum><br>Start, Stop,<br>AWC|Image preview mode<br>Allows the user to view a preview<br>image of the configuration, rather<br>than saving the image configuration<br>to the camera.<br>If this parameter is ignored, then<br>preview mode will be stopped, and the<br>original image configuration will be<br>applied.<br>• Start: Image preview mode will be<br>started<br>• Stop: Image preview mode will be<br>stopped, and the original image<br>settings saved in the camera will<br>be applied|
||Mode|REQ, RES|<enum><br>DefinitionF<br>ocus,<br>MotionFocu<br>s,<br>ReducedNo<br>ise,<br>BrightVideo<br>,<br>MotionFocu<br>s+Reduced<br>Noise,<br>MotionFocu<br>s+BrightVid<br>eo,<br>VividVideo,<br>UserPreset|Image preset mode<br>• DefinitionFocus: Puts priority on<br>definition<br>• MotionFocus: Puts priority on the<br>motion<br>• ReducedNoise: Puts priority on<br>noise reduction<br>• BrightVideo: Puts priority on the<br>brightness of the video<br>• MotionFocus+ReducedNoise: Puts<br>priority on the motion and noise<br>reduction<br>• MotionFocus+BrightVideo: Puts<br>priority on motion and brightness<br>of the video<br>• VividVideo: Puts more on noise<br>reduction and saturation|

## **Chapter 25. Imagepreset schedule**
### **25.1. Description**

The **imagepresetschedule** submenu configures the image preset mode’s scheduling.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|User|
|set|User|


### **25.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 imagepresetschedule &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **25.3. Parameters**

```















|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads settings for the image<br>preset|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
|set|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||<ddd>.<hourIndex>.E<br>nable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables Day and Hour<br>in schedule.|



SUNAPI 271


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||<ddd>.<hourIndex>.M<br>ode|REQ, RES|<csv><br>UserPreset1,<br>UserPreset2,<br>OutdoorDaytime,<br>OutdoorNightime,<br>IndoorBacklight,<br>IndoorBrightScene,<br>NumberPlate, Vivid,<br>Indoor, Outdoor|Set image preset mode in the<br>corresponding hour’s schedule.<br>**Note**<br>To use the**This parameter**,<br>**<ddd>.Enable=True,**<br>**<ddd>.<hourIndex>.Enable=**<br>**True** must be sent together.<br>**<ddd>.FromTo** must be specified<br>in the format of <hh:mm-<br>hh:mm>.<br><ddd> stands for day of the week<br>and should be specified in short<br>form such as SUN, MON, TUE,<br>WED, THU, FRI, and SAT in<br>uppercase.<br>Ex)<br>SUN.Enable=True&SUN.0.Enable=<br>True&SUN.0.Mode=Vivid|
||<ddd>.<hourIndex>.M<br>inute|REQ, RES|<csv>|**Note**<br>To use the This parameter,<br>**<ddd>.Enable=True,**<br>**<ddd>.<hourIndex>.Enable=**<br>**True** must be sent together.<br>**<ddd>.<hourIndex>.Minute**<br>value must be specified in the<br>format of <hh-hh>.<br>Ex)<br>SUN.Enable=True&SUN.0.Enable=<br>True&SUN.0.Minute=30-40|



272 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||<ddd>.Enable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enable or disable <ddd> day’s<br>scheduling.<br>**Note**<br>If this parameter is used<br>without<br>**<ddd>.<hourIndex>.Enable**<br>parameter, Enable <ddd><br>day’s all hour.<br>Ex)<br>**SUN.Enable=True** means Sunday<br>24hours.|
||Activate|REQ, RES|<enum><br>Always, Scheduled|Enable or disable scheduling.<br>• Always: Disables scheduling.<br>Apply current ImagePreset<br>mode.<br>• Scheduled: Enables<br>scheduling.|

## **Chapter 26. Thermal Palette Setting**
### **26.1. Description**

The **thermalpalettesetting** submenu configures the thermal color palette, upper&lower temperature

level, Emissivity, ColorBar overlay.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|
|set|Admin|


### **26.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 thermalpalettesetting &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **26.3. Parameters**

```













|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads settings for the image preset|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
|set|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||ThermalColorPalette|REQ, RES|<enum><br>WhiteHot,<br>BlackHot,<br>Rainbow,<br>Custom,<br>Sepia, Red,<br>Iron, Red-<br>WH, Iron-<br>WH,<br>Midrange-<br>WH|Configures thermal color palette.|



302 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||UpperTemperatureLevel|REQ, RES|<int>|Set boundary that devides high<br>temperature range and middle<br>temperature range.<br>**Note**<br>Only if**ThermalColorPalette** is<br>Red-WH,Iron-WH,Midrange-WH,<br>this parameter is applicable.<br>UpperTemperatureLevel should be<br>greater than LowerTemperatureLevel.<br>Refer**thermalpalettesettingoptions**<br>submunu for setable range.|
||LowerTemperatureLevel|REQ, RES|<int>|Set boundary that devides middle<br>temperature range and low<br>temperature range.<br>**Note**<br>Only if**ThermalColorPalette** is<br>Red-WH,Iron-WH,Midrange-WH,<br>this parameter is applicable.<br>UpperTemperatureLevel should be<br>greater than<br>LowerTemperatureLevel.<br>Refer<br>**thermalpalettesettingoptions**<br>submunu for setable range.|
||NormalizedEmissivity|REQ, RES|<int>|Configure overall<br>NormalizedEmissivity of material<br>shown in video. This configuration can<br>effect camera’s accuracy to detect<br>temperature.<br>**Note**<br>Emissivity: The**emissivity** of the<br>surface of a material is its<br>effectiveness in emitting energy<br>asthermal radiation.|
||ColorBarOverlay|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enable or disable Colorbar overlay in<br>video.|



SUNAPI 303


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||ThermalVariationSensitiv<br>ity|REQ, RES|<int>|Sensitivity of thermal variation<br>**Note**<br>This parameter applies to thermal<br>channels only<br>Attribute to check for<br>ThermalVariationSensitivity:<br>"attributes/Image/Support/[Chan<br>nleID]/<br>ThermalVariationSensitivity"|

### **26.4. Examples**




#### **26.4.1. Getting the current thermal palette settings**

REQUEST

```
 http://<IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=thermalpalettesetting&action=view

```

JSON RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK

 Content-type: application/json

 <Body>

 {
 "ThermalPaletteSetting": [
 {
 "Channel": 0,
 "ThermalColorPalette": "WhiteHot",
 "UpperTemperatureLevel": 60,
 "LowerTemperatureLevel": 20,
 "NormalizedEmissivity": 95,
 "ColorBarOverlay": false,
 "ThermalVariationSensitivity": 12
 }
 ]
 }

```

## **Chapter 28. Spot Temperature Reading**
### **28.1. Description**

The **spottemperaturereading** submenu provides the requested spot’s temperature information.


This submenu is applicable for temperature detection cameras.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|


### **28.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 spottemperaturereading &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **28.3. Parameters**

```














|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads settings for the image preset.|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||ScreenCoordinates|REQ|<string>|Request screen coordinates to display<br>temperature information.|
||Temperature|RES|<float>|Shows the spot’s current<br>temperature.|
||Unit|RES|<enum><br>Celsius,<br>Fahrenheit|Shows the current temperature’s unit.|
||ScreenResolution|REQ|<string>|Shows the current screen’s resolution.<br>**Note**<br>This parameter should be set with<br>**ScreenCoordinates**.|

## **Chapter 29. Direction Indicator**
### **29.1. Description**

The **directionindicator** submenu provides the feature to set and show options related to each direction.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|
|set|Admin|


### **29.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 directionindicator &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **29.3. Parameters**

```














|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads settings for the direction-<br>related settings.|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
||Enable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Used to request to enable or disable<br>this feature.<br>Enables or disables the direction<br>indicating feature|
||Direction|REQ, RES|<csv><br>Top,<br>TopRight,<br>Right,<br>BottomRigh<br>t, Bottom,<br>BottomLeft,<br>Left,<br>TopLeft|Users can select specific directions<br>that they want. Multiple directions are<br>allowed to set.|
|set|Channel|REQ|<int>|Channel value to set.|


310 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||Direction.#.AzimuthEnab<br>le|REQ,RES|<bool><br>True, False|Device shows an azimuth value in<br>each direction if user sets this value to<br>True.|
||Direction.#.Show|REQ,RES|<bool><br>True, False|Device shows a direction string in<br>each direction if user sets this value to<br>True.|
||Direction.#.Text|REQ,RES|<string>|User can set name for each direction<br>using this parameter.|
||Direction.#.PositionX|REQ,RES|<int>|User can specify the position of the<br>information that will appear on<br>screen.|
||Direction.#.PositionY|REQ,RES|<int>|User can specify the position of the<br>information that will appear on<br>screen.|
||ImagePreview|REQ|<enum><br>Start, Stop|Image preview mode<br>Allows viewing the preview image of<br>the configuration rather than saving<br>the image configuration to camera.|

## **Chapter 30. Noise Reduction**
### **30.1. Description**

The **noisereduct** submenu provides features related to image quality control for analog camera

connected to Hybrid NVR.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to Hybrid NVR Only.


**Access level**

|Action|NVR|
|---|---|
|view|USER|


### **30.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 noisereduct &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **30.3. Parameters**

```








|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view|Channel|RES||Channel ID|
|set|Channel|REQ||Channel ID|
||NoiseLevel|REQ,RES|<int>|Noise reduction level.<br>The higher the level value, the more<br>the noise reduction and the higher the<br>image quality.|
||HorizontalPos|REQ,RES|<int>|Horizontal position. It’s a feature for<br>screen adjustment, not for noise<br>reduction.|
||VerticalPos|REQ,RES|<int>|Vertical position. It’s a feature for<br>screen adjustment, not for noise<br>reduction.|
||HorizontalScale|REQ,RES|<int>|Horizontal scale. It’s a feature for<br>screen adjustment, not for noise<br>reduction.|
||VerticalScale|REQ,RES|<int>|Vertical scale. It’s a feature for screen<br>adjustment, not for noise reduction.|


316 Image Adjustment

### **31.1. Description**

|---|---|

 ptrpreset &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

|control|Preset|REQ|<int><br>1~4|The number of preset to be moved|
||PresetName|REQ|<string><br>initialize,<br>360, 200,<br>user|The name of preset to be moved.<br>These are predefined values and<br>match with Preset numbers in<br>sequence.|

## **Chapter 32. Stereo Calibration**
### **32.1. Description**

The **stereosensorcalibration** submenu provides commands to calibrate the coordinates of both sensors,

which is performed by superimposing a thermal image over a non-thermal image based on the scale and

adjustment of the offset.


**NOTE** This chapter is applicable to dual thermal cameras.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|
|set|Admin|


### **32.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 stereosensorcalibration &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **32.3. Parameters**

```













|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view|Channel|REQ|<csv>|Channel ID|
||HeightScale|RES|<float>|Height scale<br>Height scale between normal and<br>thermal images|
||WidthScale|RES|<float>|Width scale<br>Width scale between normal and<br>thermal images|
||ZoomScale|RES|<float>|Zoom scale<br>Zoom scale between normal and<br>thermal images|
||CalibrationCompleted|RES|<bool><br>True, False|Calibration completed<br>Shows whether calibration settings<br>are performed or not|



322 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|set|Channel|REQ,RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||CalibrationOffset|REQ,RES|<string>|Position offset of the thermal image<br>The CalibrationOffset is expressed in<br><x,y>.|

### **32.4. Examples**

 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=stereosensorcalibration&action=view&Channel=1

```

 HTTP/1.0 200 OK

 <Body>

 Channel.1.HeightScale=3.44
 Channel.1.WidthScale=3.44
 Channel.1.ZoomScale=1.22
 Channel.1.CalibrationCompleted=False
 Channel.1.CalibrationOffset=0,0

```

JSON RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK

 Content-type: application/json

 <Body>

 {
 "StereoSensorCalibration": [
 {
 "Channel": 1,

```

## **Chapter 35. BlackBody Config**
### **35.1. Description**

The **blackbodyconfig** submenu is used to configure the use of a black body device with the camera.


This chapter is applicable to dual thermal cameras.



**NOTE**


**Access level**



Attribute to check for feature support:

"attributes/Eventsource/Support/[ChannelID]/BodyTemperatureDetection"



|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|
|set|Admin|

### **35.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 blackbodyconfig &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **35.3. Parameters**

```
























|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view|Channel|REQ|<csv>|Channel ID|
||CurrentBlackBodyTempe<br>rature|RES|<float>|Current temperature of BlackBody<br>It shows the temperature of the set<br>blackbody. If Enable is false, it is<br>displayed as 0|
|set|Channel|REQ,RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||Enable|REQ,RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables BlackBody|
||Coordinates|REQ,RES|<string><br>Format=x1,<br>y1,x2,y2|Coordinates of BlackBody position<br>The coordinates are expressed in <<br>x1,y1,x2,y2>|
||EnableBlackBodyBeepAla<br>rm|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables BlackBody alarm|
||BlackBodyTemperature|REQ, RES|<float>|Temperature of BlackBody|

||BlackBodyEmissivity|REQ, RES|<int>|Emissivity of BlackBody|
||BlackBodyDistance|REQ, RES|<int>|Distance between BlackBody and<br>Camera|
||BlackBodyHeight|REQ, RES|<int>|Height of BlackBody|
||CameraHeight|REQ, RES|<int>|Height of Camera|
||CameraAngle|REQ, RES|<float>|Angle of Camera|

## **Chapter 38. Thermal NUC**
### **38.1. Description**

The **thermalnuc** submenu used in scheduling the NUC (Non Uniformity Correction).


Attribute to check for feature support:
**NOTE**

"attributes/Image/Support/[ChannelID]/ThermalNUC"


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|
|set|Admin|
|control|Admin|


### **38.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 thermalnuc &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **38.3. Parameters**

```













|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view|Channel|REQ,RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
|set|Channel|REQ|<int>|Channel ID|
||DisplayEnable|REQ,RES|<bool><br>True,False|Enable OSD when NUC is in progress.|
||DisplayPosition|REQ,RES|<enum><br>LowerLeft,L<br>owerRight,<br>UpperLeft,<br>UpperRight|OSD display position.|
||IntervalMode|REQ,RES|<enum><br>Auto,5min,1<br>5min,Sched<br>ule|Correction interval<br>**Note**<br>When the interval mode is set as<br>**Schedule**, the user can configure a<br>custom day and time to apply the<br>correction.|



344 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||<ddd>.Enable|REQ,RES|<bool><br>True, False|Day for which the schedule should be<br>enabled.<br>**Note**<br>Here, <ddd> refers to the three<br>letters of the weekday; SUN, MON,<br>TUE, etc.|
||<ddd>.<hourIndex>.Enab<br>le|REQ,RES|<bool><br>True, False|Hour of a particular day when the<br>schedule should be enabled.<br>**Note**<br>Here, <hourIndex> refers to an<br>hour index from 0 to 23|
||<ddd>.<hourIndex>.Mod<br>e|REQ,RES|<enum><br>Auto,5min,1<br>5min|Correction interval mode.|
|control|Channel|REQ|<int>|Channel ID for which the NUC should<br>be applied.|

## **Chapter 39. LP Capture Setup**
### **39.1. Description**

The **lpcapturesetup** guides you to an optimized camera installation angle when using license plate

recognition on the road. Set the camera’s height and road distance and move the camera to see the

guided values.


**NOTE** This chapter is applicable for cameras which have gyrosensor and support LPR feature.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|User|
|set|User|
|check|User|


### **39.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 lpcapturesetup &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **39.3. Parameters**

```








|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|set|CameraHeight|REQ,RES|<int>|Height(m) of camera|
||RoadDistance|REQ,RES|<int>|Vertical distance(m) between car and<br>camera. Check below picture for<br>reference|
|check|VerticalAngle|RES|<int>|Vertical angle of the current camera<br>position|
||RollAngle|RES|<int>|Rotate angle of the current camera<br>position|
||GuidedHorizontalAngle|RES|<int>|The horizontal angle at which the<br>camera should point at the vehicle.<br>This value is calculated by<br>CameraHeight, RoadDistance, and<br>VerticalAngle. Check below picture for<br>reference|


368 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view|GuidedHorizontalAngleM<br>in|RES|<int>|Minimum recommended value of the<br>horizontal angle at which the camera<br>is looking at the vehicle|
||GuidedHorizontalAngleM<br>ax|RES|<int>|Maximum recommended value of the<br>horizontal angle at which the camera<br>is looking at the vehicle|
||VerticalAngleMax|RES|<int>|Maximum recommended value of<br>camera vertical angle|
||VerticalAngleMin|RES|<int>|Minimum recommended value of<br>camera vertical angle|
||RollAngleMax|RES|<int>|Maximum recommended value of<br>camera rotation angle|
||RollAngleMin|RES|<int>|Minimum recommended value of<br>camera rotation angle|

### **39.4. Examples**

#### **39.4.1. Setting the camera height & RoadDistance**

REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=lpcapturesetup&action=set&CameraHeight=5&RoadDistance
 =5

```

SUNAPI 369


JSON RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK
 Content-type: application/json
 <Body>

 {
 "Response": "Success"
 }

#### **39.4.2. Getting current vertical angle, roll angle and guided horizontal angle**
```

REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=lpcapturesetup&action=check

```

JSON RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK
 Content-type: application/json
 <Body>

 {
 "VerticalAngle": 20,
 "RollAngle": 5,
 "GuidedHorizontalAngle": 25,
 }

#### **39.4.3. Getting setting values & recommended values range**
```

REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=lpcapturesetup&action=view

```

JSON RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK
 Content-type: application/json
 <Body>

```

 "RoadDistance": 5,
 "GuidedHorizontalAngleMin": 0,

 "VerticalAngleMax": 30,
 "RollAngleMin": 0,
 "RollAngleMax": 15,
 }

```

## **Chapter 40. WiseAutoFocus settings**
### **40.1. Description**

The **wiseautofocus** submenu configures the wiseautofocus settings.


Attribute to check for feature support:
**NOTE**

"attributes/Image/Support/[ChannelID]/WiseAutoFocus"


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|User|
|set|User|


### **40.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 wiseautofocus &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **40.3. Parameters**

```










|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view|Channel|REQ|<csv>|Channel IDs|
|set|Channel|REQ,RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||Enable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enable the wiseautofocus feature|
||QuickZoom|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True,False|Enable te quick zoom feature|
||FocusLevel|REQ,RES|<int><br>1 to 100|Focus level value.|

### **40.4. Exmaples**

#### **40.4.1. View the wiseautofocus settings**

REQUEST




```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=wiseautofocus&action=view

```

372 Image Adjustment


JSON RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK
 Content-type: application/json
 <Body>

 {
 "WiseAutoFocus": [
 {
 "Channel": 0,
 "Enable": false,
 "QuickZoom": true,
 "FocusLevel": 50
 }
 ]
 }

#### **40.4.2. set the wiseautofocus settings**
```

REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=wiseautofocus&action=set&Channel=0&Enable=True&QuickZ
 oom=True&FocusLevel=34

```

JSON RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK
 Content-type: application/json
 <Body>

 {
 "Response": "Success"
 }

```
