## **Chapter 41. AutoFocusZone Setup**
### **41.1. Description**

The **autofocuszone** submenu used to setup the autofocuszone related settings.


Attribute to check for feature support:



**NOTE**


**Access level**



"attributes/Image/Support/[ChannelID]/AutoFocusZone"

"attributes/Image/Limit/[ChannelID]/MaxAutoFocusZoneCount"



|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|
|set|Admin|
|add|Admin|
|update|Admin|
|control|Admin|
|remove|Admin|
|check|Admin|

### **41.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 autofocuszone &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **41.3. Parameters**

```



















|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view|Channel|REQ|<csv>|Channel Ids|
||Channel|RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||Enable|RES|<bool><br>True, False|If this is true, autofocuszone feature is<br>enabled|
||ZoneIndex.#.ZoneName|RES|<string>|Userconfigured zone name.|
||ZoneIndex.#.GridSize.Wi<br>dth|RES|<int>|Width of the grid.|
||ZoneIndex.#.GridSize.Hei<br>ght|RES|<int>|Height of the grid.|



374 Image Adjustment


|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||ZoneIndex.#.Cell.#.Enabl<br>e|RES|<bool><br>True,False|Cell enable state.|
|set|Channel|REQ|<int>|Channel ID|
||Enable|REQ|<bool><br>True,False|Enable or disable autofocus zone|
||ZoneIndex.#.ZoneName|REQ|<string>|Configure zone name.|
|add|Channel|REQ|<int>|Channel ID|
||ZoneIndex|REQ|<int>|Zone index to that needs to be added<br>newly.|
||ZoneName|REQ|<string>|Zonename string|
||GridSize.Height|REQ|<int>|height of the grid|
||GridSize.Width|REQ|<int>|width of the grid|
|update|Channel|REQ|<int?|Channel ID|
||ZoneIndex|REQ|<int>|Zone index to be updated|
||FocusChangedCell|REQ|<int>|Index of cell for which refocus is<br>needed.|
||DisabledCell|REQ|<int>|Index of cell for which focus needs to<br>be disabled.|
|control|Channel|REQ|<int>|Channel ID.|
||Mode|REQ|<enum><br>Move,<br>StopScanni<br>ng,<br>NoZoomMo<br>veToZone,<br>StartEdit,<br>RenewEdit,<br>ExitEdit|Control Mode|
||ZoneIndex|REQ|<int>|Zone index|
||Cell|REQ|<int>|Cell index|
||EditTimeoutSecs|RES|<int>|Timeout duration for the edit session,<br>when mode is set to StartEdit or<br>RenewEdit.|
|remove|Channel|REQ|<int>|Channel ID.|
||ZoneIndex|REQ|<int>|Zone index to be removed.|
|check|Channel|REQ|<int>|Channel ID|

|Action|Parameters|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|

||Type|REQ|<enum><br>GridSize,Sc<br>anStatus|Can check the gridsize or ScanStatus,<br>based on this parameter.|
||GridSize.MaxWidth|RES|<int>|Max width of the grid.|
||GridSize.MaxHeight|RES|<int>|Max height of the grid.|
||GridSize.MinWidth|RES|<int>|Min width of the grid.|
||GridSize.MinHeight|RES|<int>|Min height of the grid.|
||GridSize.ZoomRatio|RES|<int>|Current zoom ratio.|
||ScanStatus.SaveStatus|RES|<enum><br>None,<br>InProgress,<br>Complete|Scan status after adding or updating<br>zone|

## **Chapter 42. White LED**
### **42.1. Description**

The **whiteled** submenu configures White LED settings. White LED creates brighter and clearer images in

low illumination situations.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


Attribute to check for Feature Support: "attributes/Image/Support/WhiteLED"


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|
|set|Admin|


### **42.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/image.cgi?msubmenu=
 whiteled &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

### **42.3. Parameters**

```

||ImagePreview|REQ|<enum><br>Start, Stop|Image preview mode<br>Allows the user to view a preview<br>image of the configuration, rather<br>than saving the image configuration<br>to the camera.<br>If this parameter is ignored, then<br>preview mode will be stopped, and the<br>original image configuration will be<br>applied.<br>• Start: Image preview mode will be<br>started<br>• Stop: Image preview mode will be<br>stopped, and the original image<br>settings saved in the camera will<br>be applied.|
||Mode|REQ|<enum><br>Supplemen<br>tLight,<br>LightAlarm,<br>Off|White LED mode<br>• Off: disable White LED<br>• SupplementLight: Use White LED<br>as supplement light<br>• LightAlarm: Use White LED as light<br>alarm|
